  "short_description": {
    "id": "Cavall (Latin: Cabal) adalah anjing Raja Arthur yang dipakai dalam perburuan babi hutan raksasa Twrch Trwyth.",
    "en": "Cavall (Latin Cabal) is the hound of King Arthur that joined the chase of the giant boar Twrch Trwyth.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "cavall-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Cavall (Welsh modern: Cafall; Latin: Cabal) adalah anjing Raja Arthur, anjing kesayangannya, yang ikut memburu babi hutan raksasa Twrch Trwyth (Latin: Troynt). Dalam perburuan rusa, ia biasanya anjing terakhir yang dilepas untuk mengejar buruan, sebagaimana dalam Gereint Son of Erbin.",
      "en": "Cavall (modern Welsh Cafall; Latin Cabal) was King Arthur's favourite hound and took part in hunting the giant boar Twrch Trwyth (Latin Troynt). When a stag was hunted he was usually the final hound released after the quarry, as told in Gereint Son of Erbin.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "cavall-c01"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Historia Brittonum (abad ke-9), dalam bagian Wonders of Britain, menceritakan bahwa ketika Cabal memburu babi Troynt, jejak kakinya membekas pada batu di wilayah Buelt. Arthur lalu menumpuk gundukan batu di bawahnya yang disebut Carn Cabal; orang yang membawa batu itu pergi akan menemukannya kembali di atas gundukan keesokan harinya. Nama Buelt bertahan dalam Builth Wells, Powys.",
      "en": "The ninth-century Historia Brittonum, in its Wonders of Britain, says that while Cabal chased the boar Troynt his paw left a mark on a stone in the district of Buelt. Arthur then heaped a cairn beneath it, called Carn Cabal; anyone who carried the stone away found it back on top of the mound the following day. The name Buelt survives in Builth Wells, Powys.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "cavall-c02",
        "cavall-c03",
        "cavall-c04",
        "cavall-c08"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Dalam roman Welsh Culhwch and Olwen, Cavall ikut bersama para pemburu dan anjing lain mengejar Twrch Trwyth beserta anak-anaknya, meski perannya tidak dirinci. Sejumlah sarjana mencatat kemiripan namanya dengan kata Latin caballus (kuda); R. J. Thomas pada 1936 menganggapnya metafora yang wajar karena anjing itu kuat dan cepat.",
      "en": "In the Welsh romance Culhwch and Olwen Cavall joins the other hunters and hounds after Twrch Trwyth and its young, though his particular part is not described. Several scholars note how close the name is to Latin caballus, 'horse'; R. J. Thomas in 1936 judged it a natural metaphor for a strong, swift dog.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "cavall-c06",
        "cavall-c07"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "cavall-c01",
      "source_id": "cavall-s1",
      "quote": "was King Arthur's dog, used in the hunt for the great boar, Twrch Trwyth (Latin: Troynt, Troit ). Cavall was Arthur's \"favourite dog\", and during a stag hunt, he was customarily the last dog to be let loose to chase after the game (Gereint Son of Erbin).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cavall (Cafall, Cabal) anjing kesayangan Raja Arthur (King Arthur), dipakai memburu Twrch Trwyth (Troynt); anjing terakhir dilepas dalam perburuan rusa (Gereint Son of Erbin); tradisi Welsh.",
        "en": "Cavall (Cafall, Cabal) was King Arthur's favourite dog, used to hunt Twrch Trwyth (Troynt); last dog loosed in a stag hunt (Gereint Son of Erbin); Welsh tradition."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cavall-c02",
      "source_id": "cavall-s1",
      "quote": "the mention Arthur's dog Cabal's pawprint, preserved in rock, in the Latin tract of Historia Brittonum (9th century). The print was preserved in rock while the dog was pursuing the boar Troynt.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Historia Brittonum (abad ke-9) menyebut jejak kaki Cabal di batu saat memburu Troynt (Wonders of Britain).",
        "en": "Historia Brittonum (9th century) mentions Cabal's pawprint in rock while chasing Troynt (Wonders of Britain)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cavall-c03",
      "source_id": "cavall-s1",
      "quote": "When Cabal, who was the dog of Arthur the soldier, was hunting the boar Troynt (recté Troit), he impressed his print in the stone, and afterwards Arthur assembled a stone mound under the stone with the print of his dog, and it is called the Carn Cabal (i.e., a cairn).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cabal meninggalkan jejak di batu; Arthur menumpuk gundukan Carn Cabal di wilayah Buelt, Britania (Britain).",
        "en": "Cabal left his print in the stone; Arthur heaped the mound Carn Cabal in the Buelt region of Britain."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cavall-c04",
      "source_id": "cavall-s1",
      "quote": "And men come and remove the stone in their hands for the length of a day and a night; and on the next day it is found on top of its mound.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Batu yang dibawa pergi ditemukan lagi di atas gundukannya keesokan harinya.",
        "en": "A stone carried off is found back on its mound the next day."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cavall-c05",
      "source_id": "cavall-s2",
      "quote": "Cabal (latinskt namn; stavas ibland Caval, Cavall eller Cafal) var Kung Arturs hund, som bland annat omnämns i Historia Brittonum.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cabal (Caval, Cavall, Cafal) anjing Raja Arthur (Kung Arturs hund), disebut dalam Historia Brittonum.",
        "en": "Cabal (Caval, Cavall, Cafal) was King Arthur's dog (Kung Arturs hund), mentioned in Historia Brittonum."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cavall-c06",
      "source_id": "cavall-s1",
      "quote": "the late Welsh romance Culhwch and Olwen weaves a much more intricate tale, naming many dogs besides Cavall in the hunting party",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Roman Welsh Culhwch and Olwen menyebut banyak anjing selain Cavall dalam rombongan pemburu Twrch Trwyth.",
        "en": "The Welsh romance Culhwch and Olwen names many dogs besides Cavall in the party hunting Twrch Trwyth."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cavall-c07",
      "source_id": "cavall-s1",
      "quote": "A number of scholars have commented upon the similarity of the dog's name to the Latin word caballus for \"horse\". In an article from 1936, R. J. Thomas said that",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kemiripan nama dengan kata Latin caballus (kuda); R. J. Thomas, 1936.",
        "en": "Similarity of the name to Latin caballus (horse); R. J. Thomas, 1936."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cavall-c08",
      "source_id": "cavall-s1",
      "quote": "The \"Buelt\" name is also preserved in present-day Builth Wells (in historic county of Brecknockshire) now part of county Powys.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama Buelt bertahan dalam Builth Wells, kini bagian county Powys, Wales.",
        "en": "The name Buelt survives in Builth Wells, now in county Powys, Wales."
      }
    }

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Ceasg adalah putri duyung dalam folklor Skotlandia, bertubuh atas perempuan cantik dan berekor ikan salem muda (grilse).",
    "en": "The ceasg is a mermaid of Scottish folklore, a lovely woman above and a young salmon (grilse) below.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "ceasg-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Ceasg adalah putri duyung dalam folklor Skotlandia: tubuh bagian atasnya perempuan cantik yang menyatu dengan ekor grilse, yakni salem muda. Dalam bahasa Gaelik Skotlandia ia juga disebut maighdean na tuinne (\"gadis ombak\") atau maighdean mhara (\"gadis laut\"). Ia tinggal di laut, juga di sungai dan aliran air, dan siapa pun yang menangkapnya dapat memaksanya mengabulkan tiga permintaan.",
      "en": "The ceasg is a Scottish folklore mermaid whose upper half, that of a beautiful woman, merges into the tail of a grilse, or young salmon. In Scottish Gaelic her other names are maighdean mhara, 'sea maiden', and maighdean na tuinne, 'wave maiden'. Besides the sea she haunts rivers and streams, and whoever catches her can compel three wishes from her.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "ceasg-c01",
        "ceasg-c02",
        "ceasg-c06"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Kadang ceasg menikah dengan manusia, dan para pemandu kapal (pilot) ternama sering dikatakan keturunan dari pernikahan semacam itu. Sekalipun pernikahan berakhir dan ceasg kembali ke laut, ia tetap memperhatikan keturunannya, melindungi mereka dari badai atau menuntun mereka ke tempat ikan terbaik.",
      "en": "Ceasg sometimes wed humans, and well-known sea pilots are often said to descend from such matches. Even after such a marriage ends and she goes back to the sea, she keeps watch over her human line, shielding them in storms or leading them to the richest fishing grounds.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "ceasg-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Dalam sebagian kisah ceasg tampil lebih mengerikan: ia menelan sang pahlawan, yang tetap hidup di perutnya. Istri pahlawan memainkan harpa hingga putri duyung terpesona dan pahlawan lolos; ketika sang istri berhenti, ia yang ditelan. Atas nasihat seorang penyihir, pahlawan mencari telur berisi daya hidup putri duyung, lalu membunuhnya dengan menghancurkan telur itu. Folkloris Skotlandia Donald MacKenzie menduga ceasg semula dewi laut yang menerima korban manusia.",
      "en": "Some tales make the ceasg monstrous. She swallows the hero, who survives inside her; his wife harps until the mermaid is enchanted and he gets free, but when the music stops she swallows the wife instead. Advised by a wizard, the hero seeks an egg holding the mermaid's life, and he kills her by crushing it. Donald MacKenzie, a Scottish folklorist, proposed that she was once a sea goddess who received human sacrifices.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "ceasg-c04",
        "ceasg-c05"
      ]
    }
  ],

      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {

    },
    {
      "id": "ceasg-c02",
      "source_id": "ceasg-s1",
      "quote": "The ceasg lives not only in the sea but also in rivers and streams, and can be made to grant three wishes to anyone who captures her.",
      "locator": null,

      "statement": {
        "id": "Ceasg tinggal di laut, sungai, dan aliran air; mengabulkan tiga permintaan kepada penangkapnya.",

    {
      "id": "ceasg-c03",
      "source_id": "ceasg-s1",
      "quote": "Even when these marriages end and the ceasg returns to the sea, they will always take an interest in their human descendants, protecting them in storms or guiding them to the best fishing grounds.",

      "context": "religious-tradition",
